Title: De vita et rebus gestis Caroli S.R.E. Cardinalis, tituli S. Praxedis, Archiepiscopi Mediolani, libri septem
Creator: Carlo Bascape (Carolus a Basilicapetri, 1550-1615), Praepositus Generalis of the Clerics Regular of St Paul (Barnabites), later bishop of Novara; dedicated to Wilhelm, Count Palatine of the Rhine and Duke of Bavaria, Milan, 25 January 1592
Publisher: Ex officina typographica Davidis Sartorii (David Sartorius' press; the place of printing is not legible in the OCR title page)
Publication Date: 1592
Rights: Public Domain (published 1592; public domain by date)
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_RS-YD-dwzOoC, file bub_gb_RS-YD-dwzOoC_djvu.txt, fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session), 2026-10-16; host rights statement: licenseurl http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/ (Public Domain Mark), no possible-copyright-status field, access-restricted-item not set; contributor National Central Library of Rome (Google Books scan). A second scan of the same 1592 edition is on archive.org as ita-bnc-mag-00002365-001 (Biblioteca Nazionale Centrale di Firenze) with OCR of the same quality, and a 1602 Brescia reprint as bub_gb_EPHCwnQei48C.
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged (long s count 0, so no long-s normalisation; nothing retyped, nothing dropped, nothing corrected). A 1592 print, so public domain by date.
Content note: SECOND WITNESS, not yet quotable. The OCR of this 1592 print renders the long s as f, confuses c and e, runs words together and interleaves the printed marginal notes with the text; sampled at 10/30/50/70/90 % and found garbled throughout, though the Latin can be followed. Vendored because no cleaner scan of any edition was found (the Florence scan and the 1602 Brescia scan are no better) and no English translation exists. Seven books ("LIBER" headings), with the dedicatory epistle and a chapter index at the head. Any quotation must be verified against the page images.
---
